New file `MINING-SERVICE/.../db/changelog/report-form/changelog-processing_details_lowercase_names.xml` (2 guarded `RENAME TABLE` changesets, `sqlCheck` with `BINARY` so case-insensitive servers MARK_RAN) included last in the master — documented exception to "one table = one file" because the renames must run after the sub-form / labour-name FKs that reference the old names; entities `ProcessingDetailsForm`, `ProcessingSubForm` and the native query in `ProcessingDetailsRepository` now use the lower-case names (the native query was also broken on case-sensitive servers). - **For owner review (behaviour change):** an empty pre-Phase-5K database (tables from the old Hibernate bootstrap, no rows, no Liquibase history) used to be completed by Hibernate `update`; with Liquibase it is now refused (409) and left untouched, because Liquibase would mark the create-table changesets as ran over that schema and leave native ENUM columns (half-migrated). Such a database holds no data; the operator drops and re-creates it before a new bootstrap. Test renamed to `bootstrap_ofAnEmptyPrePhase5KDatabase_isRefused_andChangesNothing`. Please confirm or say if such databases must be converted instead.

## 2026-10-05 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P02-T11 stage D complete (REVIEW): gateway company selection from the verified `tenant_id` claim

- Scope: API-GATEWAY only (owner instruction "stage D only"). Re-read the stage D plan (inventory §3.3 / §4), developer guide §8 and CD-02 / CD-26 before editing. Backup of `API-GATEWAY/src` + `pom.xml`: agent scratchpad `backup-stage-d/` (kept). The owner approved (2026-10-05, in chat) the removal / rename of exactly 4 files; the CP, COMMON and every domain service are unchanged (checked by modification time).
- **Replaced files:** `filter/OrganizationContextFilter.java` → `filter/CompanyContextFilter.java` (old file was `A +` → `svn rm --force`), `filter/OrganizationSelection.java` → `filter/CompanySelection.java` (unversioned, deleted), `OrganizationContextFilterTest` → `CompanyContextFilterTest` (old `M +` → `svn rm --force`), `OrganizationSelectionTest` → `CompanySelectionTest` (unversioned, deleted). Local working copy only; no update, no commit.
- **Changed files:** `websocket/WebSocketTicketBinding` (`companyCode`), `WebSocketTicketController` (binds the token company, no `Active-Org`), `WebSocketTicketFilter` (ticket company as exchange attribute `erp.websocket.ticket-company`, no header), `WebSocketTicketCodec` (comment), `configs/RateLimiterConfig` (key from the token company), `configs/SecurityConfig` (comment), `application.yml` (route comments). Tests: `GatewayJwtValidationTest`, `GatewayCustomerRealmTest`, `GatewayWebSocketTicketTest`, `WebSocketTicketFilterTest`, `WebSocketTicketCodecTest`, `GatewayRateLimitingIntegrationTest`, `PublicInvoiceRouteSecurityTest`, `support/LocalJwks` (tokens carry `tenant_id`; a variant with arbitrary claims for legacy-token cases).
- **Flow:** resource server verifies the JWT (unchanged: realm issuer, signature, expiry, audience; customer tokens only on `/portal/**`) → `CompanyContextFilter` strips client `X-Company-Code`, `X-Organization-Id`, `Active-Org`, `X-Tenant-Id`, `X-Performed-By(-Id)`, customer identity headers → public / docs / actuator pass without a company; no JWT → 401; CP platform-admin routes → no company → `CompanySelection.fromToken`: `tenant_id` must be one string `CMP_<n>` (COMMON `TenantResourceNaming.isCompanyCode`), else 403 → ticket user / company must match the token (401 / 403) → `COMPANY_MODULES:<companyCode>` membership (403 not entitled or empty answer, 503 Redis error; CONTROL-PANEL and NOTIFICATION skip only the plan check) → forward with `X-Company-Code` = token company, `X-Performed-By(-Id)`, `X-User-Token`. The `groups` claim is never read; there is no fallback to the former `ORG_` group selection.
- **Security tests added / verified:** valid `tenant_id` → `X-Company-Code` = that `CMP_<n>`; missing `tenant_id` → 403; malformed / lowercase / list / number / comma list / leading space → 403; `ORG_` → 403 (and no `COMPANY_MODULES:ORG…` lookup); cross-company (token of an unentitled company + header naming an entitled one) → 403; unentitled module → 403; empty Redis answer → 403; Redis down → 503; entitled → routed; `X-Organization-Id` / `X-Company-Code` / `Active-Org` / `X-Tenant-Id` cannot override the token company (stripped; single gateway value); legacy `groups` (+ `Active-Org`) without `tenant_id` → 403, with `tenant_id` ignored; WebSocket ticket of another company → 403, of another user → 401, ticket issuance ignores `Active-Org` / `X-Company-Code`; customer realm token without `tenant_id` or with `ORG_` → 403 on the portal; contract: header and key equal COMMON `TenantContextFilter.COMPANY_CODE_HEADER` / `ModuleEntitlementEnforcementFilter.KEY_PREFIX` (= CP publisher).
- **Results:** API-GATEWAY `mvn -o clean test` → **66 run, 0 failures** (incl. the Testcontainers-Redis integration tests `GatewayJwtValidationTest` 10, `GatewayCustomerRealmTest` 5, `GatewayWebSocketTicketTest` 7, `GatewayRateLimitingIntegrationTest` 3; `CompanyContextFilterTest` 18, `CompanySelectionTest` 4). Contract / regression: COMMON 180 / 0 / 3 skipped, CP unit 180 / 0, CP ITs `InternalMetadataApiMySqlIT` 6 + `ModuleEntitlementSchedulerMySqlRedisIT` 5 + `SubscriptionLifecycleMySqlRedisIT` 6 = 17 / 0 on 192.168.5.141 + loopback Redis (pre-flight 6 protected DBs; 2 run-created DBs `control_panel_metadata_it`, `control_panel_test` dropped by exact name); unit regression COMMON-ATTACHMENT 36, ASSET 7, COMPLIANCE 178, CRM 23, FILE 12, HRMS 10, ATTENDANCE 7, LOGISTICS 7, MINING 7, PRODUCTION 11, QC 10, R&D 10, STORE 11, TICKETS 8, PAYROLL 17, CUSTOMER-PORTAL 5, NOTIFICATION 11, CHAT 16 — 0 failures; ACCOUNT-BOOK 3121 run, 30 failures + 64 errors = **KNOWN BASELINE** (identical list, not touched). Domain-service ITs not re-run (stage D changes only the gateway; their ITs do not involve it).
- **Not in stage D:** issuer → company equality against `tbl_identity_realm` and the per-company realm `co-<n>` with its hard-coded `tenant_id` mapper (stage F / P03-T5); gateway company-status check `TENANT_STATUS` (P06-T6); developer guide §8 header names (stage G).
- **Operational note (owner / operator):** until stage F, the existing staff and customer development realms need a `tenant_id` protocol mapper (`CMP_<n>`), otherwise every tenant route answers 403 (fail closed by design). No realm configuration is stored in the repository.

## 2026-10-06 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P03-T9 complete (module-aware `@RunPerTenant`, job architecture rule, ACCOUNT-BOOK e-way bill job) → REVIEW

- Owner go 2026-10-05: P03-T9 only, PHASE_03 exception (like T4 / T7); approved design G-2 … G-6 from the read-only readiness report. Not started: T8, T11. D3-10, `.env`, `_cleanup_review/` untouched.
- **COMMON (main):** `RunPerTenant.module()` (new, optional); new `tenant/job/TenantJobModule` (module resolution: annotation → `erp.tenant.job.module` → lower-cased `erp.module-entitlement.enforce.module-token`; well-formed catalog code check); `PerTenantJobAspect` (constructors take the service default module; companies only from `ControlPlaneClient.getActiveCompanyCodes(module)`; locking, per-company context, skip-on-403 and CP-outage behavior unchanged); `TenantJobAutoConfiguration` passes `TenantJobModule.resolveDefault(Environment)`; `ControlPlaneClient.getActiveCompanyCodes(@RequestParam("module"))` added, the no-arg method stays (FILE purge, G-6).
- **Fallback (G-2, fail-safe, documented in `TenantJobModule` / `RunPerTenant`):** no resolvable or a malformed module → the cycle is skipped with a WARN and the Control Plane is not called; unknown module (CP 404 — `FeignException.NotFound` or `ResourceNotFoundException`) → skipped with a WARN naming the module; empty list → nothing runs. Never a run over all ACTIVE companies. `/active?module=` = ACTIVE companies with a VERIFIED DATABASE of the module (G-5; entitlement filtering stays PHASE_06).
- **Service default modules:** catalog code = lower-case `gatewayToken` for every row of `seed/modules.yml`, and every job-running service already sets `module-token` (CRM, COMPLIANCE, HRMS, QC, TICKETS, PRODUCTION, ACCOUNT-BOOK, …) → no service YAML / Java change was needed; the architecture rule verifies each per-company job's module against the catalog seed.
- **Job architecture rule (COMMON test-jar, no new dependency):** `architecture/ScheduledJobScanner` (ASM scan of the module's compiled main classes, no class loading), `architecture/ScheduledJobPolicy` (central allow-list, every entry = module + `class#method` + kind + reason), three new tests in `CompanyTenancyArchitectureContract` (inherited by all 22 module test classes): (1) every `@Scheduled` / `@Schedules` method under `nyaalkaran.erp` has `@RunPerTenant` or an entry; (2) entries are not stale, have a reason (≥ 30 chars), runner entries really invoke `TenantJobRunner.forEachActiveCompany`; (3) per-company jobs use a catalog module with `requiresDatabase: true`. Allow-list (G-3): CP `ModuleEntitlementSyncScheduler.resyncActiveCompanies`, `IdentityReconciliationScheduler.reconcile`, `IdentitySyncWorker.processDueTasks`, `KeycloakUserCacheService.scheduledRefresh` (PLATFORM); FILE `AttachmentPurgeScheduler.purgeExpiredAttachments` (PER_COMPANY_LOOP, classification only); COMMON-ATTACHMENT `PurgeExpiredAttachmentsJob.purgeExpiredAttachments` (GATED_LIBRARY); ACCOUNT-BOOK `TenantScheduledJobs` × 5 (PER_COMPANY_RUNNER). Rule self-tests: `ScheduledJobRuleTest` 7 (fixtures incl. `@Schedules`, runner invocation, stale / unjustified / untruthful entries, unknown / database-less / malformed module).
- **ACCOUNT-BOOK (tenant plumbing only, business logic unchanged):** `TenantJobRunner` module-aware (Spring constructor resolves `account-book` from the environment; constructors `(client, module)` / `(client, lock, module)` replace the module-less ones; same fail-safe rules); `TenantScheduledJobs.processExpiredAndExpiringEwayBills` (`@Scheduled(cron = "0 0 */4 * * *")`, unchanged schedule) runs `EwayBillExpiryScheduler.processExpiredAndExpiringBills` once per company through the runner (G-4); `EwayBillExpiryScheduler` lost only its own `@Scheduled` + import (comment added; `@Transactional` business method untouched). ## 2026-10-06 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P03-T13 export permission decision + final validation (stays REVIEW)

- **Owner decision (2026-10-06):** keep `erp-realm-auditor` strictly read-only (no `manage-realm`); option 2 = a separate dedicated export client for `RealmExportJob`; determine and document the minimum Keycloak 25.0.6 permission of the partial export; if no sufficiently isolated export-only permission exists, STOP and report — no silent fallback to `manage-realm`. Export / reconciler requirements unchanged.
- **Minimum permission (source):** Keycloak 25.0.6 `RealmAdminResource.partialExport` → `auth.realm().requireManageRealm()` (`RealmPermissions.canManageRealmDefault` = `hasOneAdminRole(MANAGE_REALM)`, a plain role check, no fine-grained-authorization alternative; `partialImport` uses the same check), then `auth.groups().requireList()` (export of groups / roles) and `auth.clients().requireView()` (export of clients).
- **Minimum permission (measured, local Keycloak 25.0.6, 127.0.0.1:8081):** new gated test `LocalKeycloakRealmReconcilerIT.finding_partialExportMinimum_isManageRealm_noIsolatedExportPermission` with a temporary master client `it-realm-exporter` on the temporary realm `co-105` only: every role of the master `co-105-realm` client except `manage-realm` (17 roles incl. all `view-*` / `query-*`, `manage-clients`, `manage-users`, `manage-events`, `manage-identity-providers`, `manage-authorization`, `create-client`, `impersonation`) → 403 with and without flags; `manage-realm` alone → 200 without flags, 403 with clients + groups / roles; `manage-realm` + `view-clients` + `query-groups` → 200 (minimum); without `query-groups` → 403. The client is deleted in the test (and in `@AfterAll` as a safety net).
- **Decision outcome — STOPPED as instructed:** `manage-realm` is a write permission over the whole realm configuration (realm / token settings, authentication flows, required actions, keys and other realm components, client policies, event settings, partial import), so Keycloak 25.0.6 cannot provide an isolated export-only permission. No dedicated export client was implemented; no client was granted `manage-realm` outside the temporary measurement; `erp-realm-auditor` unchanged (view roles only). `RealmExportJob` keeps using the read-only auditor, records each realm as failed (403) and stores nothing; `REALM_EXPORT_ENABLED` stays default false. Owner to decide (e.g. accept `manage-realm` on a dedicated client with compensating controls, an offline / operator-run `kc.sh export`, or an export assembled from read-only view endpoints).
- **Changed:** `LocalKeycloakRealmReconcilerIT` (+ permission-matrix test, generic `realmClientRoles` / `realmClientRoleNames` / `partialExportStatus` helpers, exporter cleanup in `@AfterAll`); `RealmExportJob` javadoc (blocked note with the measured minimum); `keycloak/platform-realm/RUNBOOK.md` §2a (exact permission, why, measured matrix, owner decision); tracker; PHASE_03 plan; this log (+ Redis wording of the previous entry corrected); AGENT_HANDOFF; NEXT_SESSION_PROMPT.
- **Validation:** `LocalKeycloakRealmReconcilerIT` 7 / 0; focused CP unit 44 / 0 (`RealmExportTest` 13, `RealmReconcilerTest` 14, `KeycloakRealmAuditorClientTest` 6, `CompanyTenancyArchitectureTest` 11). Earlier the same day (before this change): CP unit 313 / 0, CP IT 137 / 0 — CP IT count is now 138 with the new test; only the changed IT class was re-run. No 192.168.5.15 / .52 / .53 in the run log.
- **Cleanup:** local Keycloak realms co-101, co-102, co-103, it-platform, master (co-105 deleted); master clients `it-realm-auditor`, `it-realm-exporter` and `co-105-realm` absent; `it-realm-provisioner` (P03-T11 helper) kept. MySQL: no database created by this run; final = the 6 protected. Redis: T13 does not directly use Redis; exact before/after Redis equality was not established.
- **Limitations:** MinIO — no local MinIO image (environment limitation); the export store is validated with a mocked `MinioClient` and an in-memory store only; no shared / production MinIO used or started.

## 2026-10-06 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P03-T13 final review state (export = R1 limitation) → stays REVIEW

- **Final owner decision (2026-10-06):** the Keycloak 25.0.6 permission investigation is accepted. Do not grant `manage-realm` to `erp-realm-auditor`; do not create an automated export client with `manage-realm`; do not weaken the read-only model to enable `RealmExportJob`; keep `RealmExportJob` in the code, disabled by default; automated Keycloak partial export is a documented **R1 limitation / blocker**; no view-API configuration assembly as a replacement in T13; a future automated export requires a separate owner / security-operations decision.

Operator action required on such a database (owner decision; not performed): `ALTER TABLE tbl_permission MODIFY application_id BIGINT NULL;` then give every existing row its domain (`BUSINESS` with `module_id`, or `PLATFORM` / `COMPANY_ADMIN` without), then `ALTER TABLE tbl_permission ADD CONSTRAINT ck_permission_domain_module CHECK ((permission_domain = 'BUSINESS' AND module_id IS NOT NULL) OR (permission_domain <> 'BUSINESS' AND module_id IS NULL));` — or recreate the development database (owner decision). The legacy seeder writes no permissions, so a database without hand-made permission rows has none to fix. The new tables `tbl_role` / `tbl_role_permission` get their checks normally (new tables). Fresh databases and the final PHASE_11A baseline are not affected.

## 2026-10-06 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P04-T9 complete (platform audit foundation: explicit columns, DENIED outcome, trusted client IP) → REVIEW

- **Owner approvals (2026-10-06):** P04-T9 as a PHASE_04 exception (PHASE_04 stays LOCKED); P04-T1 and P04-T2 REVIEW accepted as dependencies; T9 before T3; **P04-T3 not started** (its open decisions B–G from the readiness report remain with the owner). No unresolved T9 decision: the plan's "outcome instead of action name" was reconciled with the owner's "preserve existing PlatformAuditService behaviour" by keeping every existing action name and call and deriving their outcome.
- **Schema (`tbl_platform_audit_log`, additive; Hibernate `ddl-auto: update`; no Liquibase):** new nullable columns `occurred_at` DATETIME(6), `actor_identity_id` BIGINT (FK `fk_platform_audit_log_actor_identity_id` → `tbl_identity`), `actor_name` VARCHAR(100), `actor_scope` VARCHAR(20) (PLATFORM / COMPANY / SYSTEM), `outcome` VARCHAR(20) (SUCCESS / DENIED / FAILED), `permission_code` VARCHAR(120), `company_id` BIGINT (FK `fk_platform_audit_log_company_id` → `tbl_company`), `detail_json` JSON (before / after or metadata, CD-28 item 2), `correlation_id` VARCHAR(64); indexes `idx_platform_audit_company_occurred`, `idx_platform_audit_actor_occurred`, `idx_platform_audit_action_occurred`. Existing columns (`actor_id`, `action`, `target_type`, `target_id`, `ip_address`, audit columns) unchanged. All new columns nullable on purpose: a NOT NULL addition to a populated table would fail under MySQL strict mode and `update` swallows the DDL error. Rows written before P04-T9 keep only the old columns.
- **Code (CP):** new enums `AuditOutcome`, `AuditActorScope`; `model/PlatformAuditLog` (columns above); new `security/TrustedClientAddress` (forwarding headers believed only from `erp.security.trusted-proxies` — IPs / CIDR; `X-Forwarded-For` right-most untrusted hop, `X-Real-IP` only without XFF, IP literals only, no DNS, canonical form — same rules as the gateway limiter of P03-T12); `security/PlatformActorResolver` (+ `currentActor()` = name + `tbl_identity` id + scope by the token's ACTIVE registry realm and subject, lookup failures never break the audit; `currentIpAddress()` through `TrustedClientAddress`; `currentCorrelationId()` from a well-formed `X-Correlation-Id` or the MDC; `currentActorId()` and the no-argument constructor unchanged); `service/PlatformAuditService` (explicit constructors instead of Lombok; existing `record(action, …)` / `recordForActor(…)` unchanged in behaviour and now fill occurred_at, actor name / identity / scope, outcome, IP, correlation id; new `record(AuditEntry)` and `recordDenied(action, permissionCode, companyId, targetType, targetId, detail)` — DENIED / FAILED in their own transaction (`REQUIRES_NEW`, survive the refused request's rollback), SUCCESS joins the caller's transaction; secret-like detail keys (password, secret, token, credential, authorization, cookie, private key) redacted recursively; long values truncated to the column limits; never throws); `application.yml` `erp.security.trusted-proxies: ${ERP_SECURITY_TRUSTED_PROXIES:}` (default empty = peer address only). No denial is wired yet (no `@RequiresPlatformPermission`; that is P04-T3).

## 2026-10-06 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P04-T3 complete (platform permission enforcement v2, default v1) → REVIEW

- **Owner approval (2026-10-06):** P04-T3 as a PHASE_04 exception (PHASE_04 stays LOCKED); P04-T9, P04-T1 / T2 and P03-T2 / T4 REVIEW accepted as dependencies (none modified); decisions A (every DENIED decision audited via T9), B (v2 only on `/api/v1/platform/admin/**`; internal `/api/v1/internal/platform/**` keeps the P03-T7 M2M / azp model), C (endpoint → permission mapping), D (`@PlatformSuperAdminOnly` endpoints delegable through v2; archive stays Super Admin, P04-T5), E (DISABLED templates grant nothing; Super Admin implicit-all and scope bypass independent of grants; v1 default and kept), F (`/api/v1/platform/companies/{code}/**` unchanged, PHASE_10), G (LISTED list filtering deferred to P04-T5).
- **New (CP `platform/security`):** `PlatformAccessResolverV2` — fail-closed decision from the verified token only: JWT principal → ACTIVE **PLATFORM** registry realm of the issuer (company-realm tokens → NOT_PLATFORM_TOKEN) → ACTIVE PLATFORM identity of the subject with an ACTIVE `tbl_platform_admin` row (else NO_ACTIVE_PLATFORM_ADMIN) → **Super Admin** = admin type SUPER_ADMIN **and** realm role `PLATFORM_SUPER_ADMIN` (master §8.1): ALLOW for every permission and company without any `tbl_role_permission` row and regardless of the template status → otherwise an ACTIVE SYSTEM PLATFORM role definition (scope 0) named by one of the token's realm roles must grant the required ACTIVE PLATFORM permission (else PLATFORM_PERMISSION_DENIED; DISABLED role / DEPRECATED permission / client roles never count) → with a path company: scope ALL, or LISTED with the company in `tbl_platform_admin_company_scope` (else COMPANY_OUT_OF_SCOPE; unknown or malformed codes are out of scope); result carries the company id for the audit. `PlatformAccessDecision` (ALLOW, UNAUTHENTICATED, NOT_PLATFORM_TOKEN, NO_ACTIVE_PLATFORM_ADMIN, PLATFORM_PERMISSION_DENIED, COMPANY_OUT_OF_SCOPE; error codes `PLATFORM_PERMISSION_DENIED` / `COMPANY_OUT_OF_SCOPE`), `PlatformAccessDeniedException` (COMMON `ApiException`, 403 with the code), `RequiresPlatformPermission` (`value`, `company` path variable), `PlatformAccessModel` (v1 / v2, blank → v1, anything else refused at startup).
- **Changed (CP):** `security/PlatformSecurityInterceptor` — with v2, a handler annotated `@RequiresPlatformPermission` and reached on `/api/v1/platform/admin/**` is decided by v2 **instead of** the coarse `@PlatformAdminOnly` / `@PlatformSuperAdminOnly` checks (decision D delegation); a refusal is audited exactly once (`recordDenied`: action `PLATFORM_ACCESS`, permission, company id, target `ENDPOINT` "METHOD path", detail decision / error code / company code; own transaction, T9) and thrown as 403; v1 (default), the internal mapping and unannotated handlers run the former checks unchanged; the 2-argument constructor (v1) is kept; v2 without its collaborators refuses to start. Annotations (decision C) on all 18 admin endpoints: `PlatformCompanyController` (list, get, provisioning-status → `platform.company.read`; suspend / disable / reactivate → `platform.company.lifecycle`), `PlatformOnboardingController` (create → `platform.company.create`), `PlatformProvisioningController` (provision / recover / reset → `platform.company.provision`), `PlatformSubscriptionController` (get → `platform.subscription.read`, the other 7 → `platform.subscription.manage`); the coarse annotations stay. `application.yml` `platform.access.model: ${PLATFORM_ACCESS_MODEL:v1}`. CP `AGENTS.md`; PHASE_04 plan T3 status. `PlatformSecurityConfig` unchanged: the user chain already ends with `anyRequest().denyAll()` and the legacy admin APIs are listed explicitly in the service chain (P03-T4 / T7 / T12).
- **Not done (owner decisions / later tasks):** LISTED filtering of list results and company archive (P04-T5); company self-service routes (PHASE_10); role activation / assignment API (P04-T4 — the seeded templates are DISABLED, so with v2 only the Super Admin has access until roles are activated). **Known limitation:** endpoints without a company in the path (company list, onboarding create, subscriptions by id) are decided on the permission only; a LISTED admin is not scope-checked there until P04-T5.
- **Tests (new):** `security/PlatformAccessResolverV2Test` 11 (decision table: Super Admin allowed everywhere without grants and needs row + realm role; normal admin with grant + scope; missing permission / empty base role; DISABLED role and DEPRECATED permission; LISTED in / out of scope / unknown / malformed company; suspended admin, inactive or company-scoped identity, missing admin row; company-realm and foreign issuer tokens with platform role names; inactive platform realm; client roles + `tenant_id` claim ignored; missing / non-JWT authentication; error codes), `security/PlatformAdminEndpointMatrixTest` 8 (the 4 real controllers in MockMvc behind the interceptor: every endpoint asks v2 for its mapped permission and path company and passes when allowed; refusal = 403 + code + exactly one audit + no service call even with a coarse Super Admin authority; COMPANY_OUT_OF_SCOPE code; Super-Admin-only endpoint delegated; client company / role headers never replace the path company; internal path keeps the coarse checks and never consults v2; v1 former behaviour on both paths; flag default v1, invalid value refused, v2 without collaborators refused), `security/PlatformAccessV2MySqlIT` 4 (192.168.5.141 only, isolated DB `control_panel_platform_access_it`, run-unique data: real P04-T2 seed with `PLATFORM_SUPPORT` activated in the test DB, real admin rows and LISTED scope, real resolver + interceptor + T9 audit — Super Admin allowed without grants and no audit row; LISTED support admin: own company allowed, other company / missing permission refused with exactly one DENIED row each (actor identity, IP, permission, company, decision); DISABLED template, suspended admin and company-realm token refused with their decisions; denial row kept and exactly once while the request transaction (with its own data change) rolls back; the IT passed twice on the same DB). 401 for anonymous calls on both prefixes stays covered by `PlatformSecurityChainTest` (unchanged, green).
- **Rollback-test fix (test only, before the first run):** the first version of the rollback test changed company B inside the outer request transaction while the refused call targeted company B; the DENIED row (own `REQUIRES_NEW` transaction) carries `company_id` = B as a foreign key, so its insert would have waited on the outer transaction's row lock (lock-wait timeout, row lost). ## 2026-10-06 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P04-T6 complete (module catalog services, cycle detection, internal module API) → REVIEW

- **Owner go (2026-10-06):** "P04-T6 IMPLEMENTATION — START NOW" under the recorded decisions T6-1..T6-12 (PHASE_04 exception T6-1, PHASE_04 stays LOCKED). Tracker row confirmed before any change (remaining scope IN_PROGRESS 2026-10-06; E-A stays REVIEW).
- **Catalog (CP `platform/catalog`, new):** `ModuleCatalogService` — DRAFT create (formats: code `[a-z][a-z0-9-]`, gateway token `[A-Z][A-Z0-9-]`, db token `[a-z][a-z0-9_]`, service name `[A-Z][A-Z0-9-]`; unique code / gateway token / db token / service name → 409 `DUPLICATE_RECORD`), DRAFT edit of every field except the code, DRAFT delete only when no dependency references it (409 `MODULE_IN_USE`), DRAFT → ACTIVE (a module that needs a database needs a `db_token`) → RETIRED, RETIRED → ACTIVE refused (409 `INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION`, T6-10), ACTIVE: only `name`, `description`, `category`, `sort_order`; a different `module_type`, provisioning flag, `db_token`, `gateway_token` or `service_name` → 409 `MODULE_FIELD_IMMUTABLE` (T6-11), RETIRED read-only; every mutation under the module's row lock. `ModuleDependencyService` — add: self-reference 400 `DEPENDENCY_SELF_REFERENCE` before any database access (T6-8), CONDITIONAL needs (and only it may carry) a `conditionSettingKey`, existing ACTIVE pair 409 `DEPENDENCY_EXISTS`, a removed pair is re-activated (the pair stays unique), DFS from the module depended on over **every ACTIVE edge** (MANDATORY, RECOMMENDED, CONDITIONAL — T6-12) → 409 `DEPENDENCY_CYCLE` with the cycle path; remove: the edge becomes RETIRED, MANDATORY only with `confirmMandatoryRemoval = true` (409 `DEPENDENCY_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED`); impact preview: companies holding any resource of the dependent module ∪ ACTIVE companies currently entitled to it (`ModuleEntitlementService`), `bundleImpact = DEFERRED_TO_PHASE_05` (T6-5 / T6-6). **Concurrency:** every dependency change first takes the catalog-wide lock `ModuleRepository.lockAllForCatalogChange` (every `tbl_module` row, `FOR UPDATE`, id order — the first statement of the transaction) and reads the ACTIVE edges with a locking read (`FOR SHARE`), so concurrent inserts are serialized and the second sees the first; per-pair locks would not be enough (disjoint pairs can close a cycle together). `CatalogAudit` — SUCCESS (joins the transaction) / FAILED (own transaction) with `platform.catalog.manage`, target `MODULE` / `MODULE_DEPENDENCY`, before / after or attempted values + error code. `CatalogException` (codes above).
- **APIs (CP):** `controller/PlatformCatalogController` — `/api/v1/platform/admin/catalog/modules` (GET list, GET `{code}`, POST create, PATCH `{code}`, POST `{code}/activate|retire`, DELETE `{code}`) and `/api/v1/platform/admin/catalog/dependencies` (GET list, GET `{id}/impact`, POST add, POST `{id}/remove`) (T6-9); reads `platform.catalog.read` + v1 `@PlatformAdminOnly`, changes `platform.catalog.manage` (not grantable) + v1 `@PlatformSuperAdminOnly`. `controller/internal/InternalModuleController` — `GET /api/v1/internal/platform/modules`: ACTIVE and RETIRED modules (never DRAFT) with code, tokens, service name, type, status, provisioning flags; `@InternalServiceOnly` + the service chain's existing `default` M2M azp allow-list (no dedicated list, T6-7); `Cache-Control: max-age=60, private`, strong `ETag` (SHA-256 of the content), `If-None-Match` → 304; no Redis. DTOs `CreateModuleRequest`, `UpdateModuleRequest`, `AddModuleDependencyRequest`, `RemoveModuleDependencyRequest`, `ModuleResponse`, `ModuleDependencyResponse`, `DependencyImpactResponse`, `InternalModuleResponse`.
- **Changed (CP):** `model/ModuleDependency` + `@Check ck_module_dependency_not_self` (`module_id <> depends_on_module_id`) — created only in tables Hibernate creates from now on; `ddl-auto: update` does not add it to an existing table, so existing databases rely on the service rule (not claimed retroactive, T6-8); `ModuleRepository` (+ `findByDbToken`, `findByCodeForUpdate`, `lockAllForCatalogChange`, ordered finders), `ModuleDependencyRepository` (+ pair finder, `isReferenced`, locking ACTIVE-edge read, fetch-join finders), `CompanyResourceRepository` (+ `findCompanyCodesByModuleId`); `seed/modules.yml` comments only (T6-2 `chat` BUSINESS, T6-3 all CHARGED, T6-4 MANDATORY confirmed — the values were already seeded, unchanged); CP `AGENTS.md`; PHASE_04 plan T6 status. No other schema change, no Liquibase, no `application.yml`, gateway, Redis, Keycloak or consumer change; `ModuleCatalog` untouched (T7).
- **Tests (new):** `catalog/ModuleCatalogServiceTest` 11, `catalog/ModuleDependencyServiceTest` 12 (self-reference before any database access, direct / transitive / RECOMMENDED / CONDITIONAL cycles, RETIRED edges ignored, lock before the edge read (in order), exists / re-activation, condition key rule, impact union, MANDATORY confirmation, RECOMMENDED removal, cycle path), `security/PlatformCatalogEndpointTest` 5 (11 endpoints × v1 / v2, 409 cycle answer, internal token + `Cache-Control` / `ETag` / 304), `security/InternalModuleApiSecurityTest` 2 (real security chains: `default` list admits only its client, other clients / user / staff / company tokens refused, no token 401; without a `default` list nobody passes); test harness `ControlPlaneSecurityHarness` + modules probe and an extra-properties constructor. IT: `catalog/ModuleCatalogMySqlIT` 6 (192.168.5.141 only, DB `control_panel_module_catalog_it`, Spring-managed services: seed holds the 19 modules, 14 dependencies, `chat` BUSINESS, all CHARGED, the three MANDATORY, acyclic; the new-database check exists and refuses a self-reference row (MySQL 3819); DRAFT CRUD / lifecycle / immutability with SUCCESS / FAILED rows; self-reference and direct / transitive (over RECOMMENDED and CONDITIONAL edges) cycles refused with the ACTIVE edge count unchanged; MANDATORY removal guard, impact from a company resource, re-activation without a second row; internal API content and headers; concurrent inserts that would together close a cycle (3 rounds of disjoint pairs + the direct pair) → exactly one wins, the stored graph stays acyclic) — passed twice on the same DB; `ModuleCatalogSeederMySqlIT` 3 / 3 with the new check.
- **Test-side fixes during the work:** (1) the self-reference row through JDBC is reported by Spring as `UncategorizedSQLException` (MySQL 3819), not `DataIntegrityViolationException` — the IT asserts `DataAccessException` + the constraint name; (2) a refused RETIRED → ACTIVE attempt writes its own FAILED `CATALOG_MODULE_ACTIVATED` row — the IT counts SUCCESS and FAILED separately. Production code unchanged by both.

gateway-token parity for existing modules preserved; DRAFT never an active / usable entitlement module. **D7-5** `TenantProvisioningService` selects `requires_database = true` AND `requires_bootstrap = true` AND status ACTIVE or RETIRED, ordered by the catalog `sort_order`; the YAML list is no longer authoritative. **D7-6** `tenant.provisioning.modules` NOT deleted in T7 while PHASE_08 does not read the catalog; T7 stops using it as the runtime source; config kept until the PHASE_08 catalog-read boundary; the static acceptance rule must not require its premature deletion. **D7-7** `SlugPolicy` reserves ACTIVE + RETIRED catalog codes (DRAFT not required), lazy lookup, no constructor-time DB access. **D7-8** static acceptance = word-boundary match for actual legacy `ModuleCatalog` class use, not `ModuleCatalogService` / `ModuleCatalogSeeder` nor allowed documentation / comments; - **Implemented (CP):** new `platform/catalog/CatalogConsistencyCheck` — startup `@EventListener(ApplicationReadyEvent)` `@Order(ORDER)` with `ORDER = ModuleCatalogSeeder.ORDER + 1000` (after the catalog seed and `BusinessRoleCatalogSeeder`), gated by `erp.catalog-consistency.enabled`; `run()` reads all `tbl_module` rows (read-only, never writes, catches every RuntimeException → one WARN, returns the findings). Rules: (1) catalog — only what P04-T6 does not enforce: `code` = lower-case `gateway_token`, ACTIVE module needs `service_name`, one usable module per `service_name` (T6 checks it in the service only, no DB key); (2) Eureka — existing `DiscoveryClient` via `ObjectProvider`; `getServices()` compared case-insensitively; ACTIVE service not registered = WARN, RETIRED = INFO, DRAFT never a runtime module; registered service that is no usable module = WARN (INFO when a DRAFT module names it); the own service counts as registered (not visible right after startup); no client / empty registry (Eureka disabled, unreachable, not fetched yet) / failing client = one WARN "Eureka check skipped", never a failure; (3) Gateway — static `gatewayDrift(modules, routes)`: the route with path prefix `/<lower-case gateway_token>/**` must exist (WARN / INFO) and target `lb://<service_name>` (case-insensitive); a route prefix outside the usable catalog = WARN (INFO for a DRAFT token); route ids are not compared; used only by the build-time tests (no runtime Gateway call, option C). Ignore-list (T8-7, exact names, no wildcard): `IGNORED_SERVICES` = `API-GATEWAY`, `DISCOVERY-SERVER`; `IGNORED_ROUTES` = `login-discovery`. Output: `Catalog drift [check] subject: message` WARN / INFO lines + one summary line. New `CatalogConsistencyScheduler#verify` — `@Scheduled(cron = "${erp.catalog-consistency.cron:0 47 * * * *}")`, `@SchedulerLock(name = "CONTROL-PANEL-SERVICE:catalog-consistency", lockAtMostFor = "PT10M", lockAtLeastFor = "PT1M")`, `@ConditionalOnProperty(erp.catalog-consistency.enabled, matchIfMissing = true)`, failures caught (WARN). `application.yml`: `erp.catalog-consistency.enabled: ${CATALOG_CONSISTENCY_ENABLED:true}`, `cron: ${CATALOG_CONSISTENCY_CRON:0 47 * * * *}`.

ALREADY_REGISTERED → `PLATFORM_SUPER_ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_ALREADY_REGISTERED`, target `PLATFORM_ADMIN` / existing platform admin id, SUCCESS (idempotent no-op), detail `{allowAdditional: <current>}`. BOOTSTRAPPED (registrar) and FAILED unchanged. Actor `PlatformAdminBootstrapRegistrar.ACTOR` = `system:platform-bootstrap` through `recordForActor(AuditEntry, ACTOR)` (explicit outcome; actor_scope SYSTEM automatically); permission_code / company_id null; no username, e-mail, password or secret in the detail.

Plus the owner's T6 scope confirmation (no schema change, no Liquibase, not T4 / T5 / T7 / PHASE_07).
- **Second readiness check (after re-reading the tracker, NEXT_SESSION_PROMPT §0 and the plan): NOT READY.** Two rules are not resolved by TX-1..TX-11 and would otherwise have to be invented:
  - **TX-12 — tax-rule identity:** the "rule key" of the one-APPROVED-per-date rule and of the TX-1 supersede is either the `code` (CD-25 "one APPROVED version per object and date"; TX-10 numbering per code) or the applicability + supply type (PHASE_01 `tbl_tax_rule` "exactly one APPROVED rule per (applicability, supply type, date)"); they diverge when the versions of one code change `sac_code` / `plan_type` / `supply_type` or two codes share an applicability. Recommendation: applicability and `supply_type` are fixed per code (set by the first version, 409 on a different value); two codes with the same applicability + supply type may not have overlapping APPROVED windows (409); TX-1 supersedes within the code.
  - **TX-13 — SAMPLE use outside production:** PHASE_05 plan ("approved (or SAMPLE) tax configuration") and PHASE_07 plan ("APPROVED (or SAMPLE in dev)", watermark when a SAMPLE / non-APPROVED version is used) imply that DRAFT SAMPLE rows are used outside production, but no rule says when the resolver may select them, and the SAMPLE rows need an `effective_from` (NOT NULL). Recommendation: outside `prod` / `production` only, when no APPROVED version covers the date, the resolver may use the DRAFT `SAMPLE-*` versions covering the date and flags the result `sample = true`; production uses APPROVED only; SAMPLE rows `effective_from` = seeding date, no `effective_to`; codes and the seller `legal_name` named `SAMPLE-*`.

- **Design points within TX-1..TX-14 (for the owner review):** (1) approvals lock their scope first (tax rule: the code + the same identity; seller: all versions; billing policy: all versions) — concurrent approvals serialise and exactly one wins; (2) the TX-1 supersede is written as a `*_UPDATED` SUCCESS row with `reason = SUPERSEDED` / `supersededBy`; the approval row carries `superseded`; (3) in production the resolver calls the gate first, so production resolution needs the full APPROVED configuration (seller, billing policy, INTRA + INTER rule) — 409 `TAX_CONFIGURATION_NOT_APPROVED`; (4) TX-13 literal reading applied: any APPROVED tax rule valid on the date disables the SAMPLE rules for that date, for every supply type; (5) a result using the SAMPLE seller with an APPROVED rule is `sample = true`; (6) `PUT` replaces all fields of a DRAFT; the code is the path and must be empty in the body; (7) concurrent creates of the same code / policy version end in the unique key → 409 `DuplicateRecordException` (retry); (8) the resolver has no runtime consumer in T6 (P05-T5 / PHASE_07); (9) SAC codes are checked for length (≤ 10) only.
- **Tests:** focused unit 44 / 0 (first run); `TaxConfigurationMySqlIT` 7 / 0 twice on 192.168.5.141 (first run green); full unit reactor (`mvn -o -fae clean install`, 24 modules): COMMON 287 / 0, API-GATEWAY 128 / 0, **CP 632 / 0** (588 + 44), every other module 0 failures, ACCOUNT-BOOK 3136 / 30 F + 64 E — **94 names identical** (baseline file minus its 4 CP `IdentitySyncMySqlIT` entries); **CP IT 221 / 0 / 0** (214 + 7; local-Keycloak 25.0.6 ITs and Redis `erp-it-redis` included); **0** references to 192.168.5.15 / .52 / .53 in every run log. Note: the reactor took 1:58 h because the workstation entered Modern Standby 19:20:45–21:10:35 (System log Kernel-Power 506 / 507) during PRODUCTION-SERVICE's context test (6 / 0) — environment only; the CP IT ran with a keep-awake hold.
- **Cleanup:** MySQL 192.168.5.141 pre-flight = the 6 protected databases (+ `control_panel_tax_it` from the focused IT); 57 run-created databases dropped by exact name; back to information_schema, mysql, performance_schema, sakila, sys, world. Redis `erp-it-redis`: 0 keys before and after. Keycloak test realms untouched. P05-T6 does not directly use Redis; exact before/after Redis equality was not established beyond the key count (0 / 0).
- **Status:** P05-T6 → **REVIEW** (NOT DONE). P05-T4 / T5 PENDING; P05-T7 → P06-T8; PHASE_05 UNLOCKED 3 / 7.   - **S6-13 (period date arithmetic):** (1) month-end: S6-8 "start + 1 month − 1 day" with Java semantics gives 31 Jan → `plusMonths(1)` = 28 Feb → end 27 Feb, and the next period starting 28 Feb drifts the cycle (28 Feb → 27 Mar → …); (2) the date source / time zone of the activation date and all subscription dates (S6-7 fixes `Asia/Kolkata` for numbering only); (3) where a renewal period starts when later periods are already paid ahead. **Recommendation:** all subscription dates are `LocalDate` in `Asia/Kolkata` from an injectable `Clock`; the activation date = the `Asia/Kolkata` date of the `activateOnFullPayment` / `applyRenewal` call; the cycle is **anchored** on the first period start (reset to the reactivation date after an EXPIRED renewal, S6-10): period n starts at anchor `plusMonths(n − 1)` / `plusYears(n − 1)` (Java clamps to the month end) and ends the day before period n + 1 starts (anchor 31 Jan → 31 Jan–27 Feb, 28 Feb–30 Mar, 31 Mar–29 Apr; anchor 29 Feb → 28 Feb in non-leap years); a renewal adds the period after the last existing period (no gap, no overlap). Alternative: the literal chained rule (next start = previous end + 1, accepting the drift).
  - **S6-14 (allowed operations per status):** **Recommendation:** PENDING — activation (NEW order) and cancel only; ACTIVE — UPGRADE, DOWNGRADE, RENEWAL, suspend, cancel, schedule-cancel; SUSPENDED — RENEWAL (stays SUSPENDED, S6-3), reinstate, **cancel (adds SUSPENDED → CANCELLED, not in the S6-3 table — needs a yes / no)**; EXPIRED — RENEWAL only (no cancel; EXPIRED → CANCELLED not added); CANCELLED — nothing; any other combination → 409 `INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION`.
  - **S6-15 (`renewal_date`):** the existing column has no defined value in the order-driven model. **Recommendation:** `renewal_date` = `end_date` + 1 day (the day the next period would start), set at activation and at every renewal, NULL while PENDING; unchanged by suspend / cancel. Alternative: always NULL in R1 (manual renewal only).

## 2026-10-09 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P06-T2 implementation paused: blocking owner question S6-16 (progress note; no code written)

- **Found during the implementation design (before any code):** S6-1 defines `FulfilledOrder` with priced `lines` only, and S6-9 says a DOWNGRADE ends the *affected* items at the period end and creates the new items SCHEDULED. The approved P05-T5 engine (`PricingEngine.downgrade`, A5-7) represents a DOWNGRADE without priced lines: `PricingResult.lines` is empty, the change is `scheduledRemovals` (plan codes removed at the period end) plus `targetUsers` / `targetStorageGb` (requested totals), effective the day after the period end. The approved input record therefore cannot express a downgrade, and "affected items" / the price snapshot of a reduced users / storage item are not defined.
- **Owner question S6-16 (options):** (a) extend the PHASE_06-owned `FulfilledOrder` with the engine DOWNGRADE data (removed plan ids, target users, target storage GB): the PLAN items of the removed plans end at the current period end; the ADDITIONAL_USERS / ADDITIONAL_STORAGE items end and new SCHEDULED items with the reduced quantity start the next day, keeping the current item's price snapshot (no new price, no refund, Q-08); DEPENDENCY items end only when their module plan is in the removal list (no automatic pruning in T2) — **recommended**; (b) a DOWNGRADE order carries the full next-period configuration as priced lines (all current items end, all lines become SCHEDULED) — conflicts with the approved engine output (no priced lines); (c) T2 only records DOWNGRADE_SCHEDULED with the order reference, the item change is applied later by T3 / a renewal order.
- **State:** P06-T2 stays IN_PROGRESS (claimed 2026-10-09), paused; no code, test, configuration or schema change; tracker unchanged 39 / 0 / 1 / 3 / 1 / 98. Backup: agent scratchpad `backup-p06-t2-paused/`.
- **Documentation encoding fix (same entry):** two em-dashes in the tracker rows P05-T3 and P06-T1 were double-encoded (`â€”` instead of `—`) by earlier result texts passed to perl through `%ENV` (P05-T3 and P06-T1 REVIEW updates); the byte sequence was corrected to `—` in those two places only — no wording changed.

## 2026-10-09 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P06-T2 S6-16 recorded; implementation paused on S6-17 / S6-18 (progress note; no code written)

- **Owner decision:** "OWNER DECISION — S6-16: DOWNGRADE INPUT" — option (a) approved with exact rules; recorded in PHASE_06 plan §3c: `FulfilledOrder` extended with the plan ids to remove, the target user count and the target storage GB (P05-T5 `PricingEngine.downgrade`: `scheduledRemovals`, `targetUsers`, `targetStorageGb`); PLAN items of the removal list and the existing ADDITIONAL_USERS / ADDITIONAL_STORAGE items end at the current period's end; replacement items with the reduced quantities are SCHEDULED from the following day with the old price snapshots (no new price, no refund, Q-08); DEPENDENCY items end only when their module plan is in the removal list; engine semantics followed as they are.
- **New ambiguity found while mapping S6-16 onto the data model (before any code) — owner questions:**
  - **S6-17 (users / storage replacement):** (1) S6-9 makes every upgrade increase of users / storage a **new row with its own price snapshot**, so one subscription period can hold several ADDITIONAL_USERS (or ADDITIONAL_STORAGE) rows with different unit prices; S6-16 says "preserve the old items' price snapshots" but not which snapshot the reduced quantity keeps when several exist. (2) The engine target is a **total** (`targetUsers` = included + additional; `targetStorageGb` = included + blocks × block size, master §12.2 steps 6 / 7, S5-10), while the item quantity is the **additional** users / **blocks**; the included amounts after the downgrade must come from somewhere. **Recommendation:** (1) the reduction removes the most recently added quantity first (last in, first out): rows are kept in creation order until the reduced quantity is reached, the last kept row may be cut to the remainder; every replacement row keeps the snapshot of the row it replaces; (2) included users / GB are taken from the price snapshots the subscription already references — the `included_users` / `included_storage_gb` of the plan prices of the items that remain after the removal plus the base of the USER_SEAT / STORAGE price referenced by the existing ADDITIONAL_* row (block size = its `unit_quantity`); additional = max(0, target − included), blocks = ceil(max(0, target − included) / block size); a target that would need more than the current quantity is refused (400 `INVALID_SUBSCRIPTION_ORDER`, the engine already forbids increases). Alternatives: (1) keep only the snapshot of the oldest (or newest) row for the whole reduced quantity; (2) carry the target additional users / blocks directly in `FulfilledOrder` instead of totals.
  - **S6-18 (second downgrade in one period):** after a scheduled downgrade the affected items already end at the period end and SCHEDULED replacements exist; a second DOWNGRADE order in the same period has no defined base. **Recommendation:** at most one scheduled downgrade per period — a second DOWNGRADE before the period end → 409 `INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION`; an UPGRADE after a scheduled downgrade stays allowed (adds ACTIVE items now). Alternative: apply the second downgrade to the configuration scheduled for the next period (the earlier SCHEDULED replacements are ended and recomputed).
- **State:** P06-T2 IN_PROGRESS (claimed), paused; no code, test, configuration or schema change; tracker unchanged 39 / 0 / 1 / 3 / 1 / 98. Docs: plan §3c + T2 status, tracker P06-T2 note, this WORK_LOG. Backup: agent scratchpad `backup-p06-t2-s616/`.

## 2026-10-09 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P06-T2 S6-17 / S6-18 recorded; readiness NOT READY (S6-19) (progress note; no code written)

- **Owner decisions:** "OWNER DECISIONS — S6-17 and S6-18" — recorded in PHASE_06 plan §3c: S6-17 LIFO reduction, one replacement row per original row with its own snapshot (never merged), included users / storage from the remaining applicable plan-item snapshots, additional = max(0, target − included), blocks = ceil(max(0, target − included) / `unit_quantity`), an increasing target → 400 `INVALID_SUBSCRIPTION_ORDER`, no new pricing / refund; S6-18 one scheduled downgrade per period, another before the period end → 409 `INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION`, UPGRADE after a scheduled downgrade allowed.
- **Readiness review (read-only, checked against the schema and the P05-T5 engine): NOT READY — owner question S6-19 (base included amount and block size):**
  - Evidence: `PricingEngine` adds the base included users / GB of the USER_SEAT / STORAGE plan once (NEW: from the ACTIVE USER_SEAT / STORAGE plan; UPGRADE / DOWNGRADE: from the explicit input `current.includedUsers()` / `current.includedStorageGb()`) and creates an ADDITIONAL_USERS / ADDITIONAL_STORAGE line only when additional > 0. The subscription therefore references a USER_SEAT / STORAGE price snapshot only through an ADDITIONAL row: (1) a subscription without such a row has no snapshot from which S6-17 could derive the base (and its current total for the "no increase" check); (2) several ADDITIONAL rows can reference different USER_SEAT / STORAGE price snapshots (the plan republished between the purchase and an upgrade) with different base amounts and block sizes (`unit_quantity`), so "included" and "blocks" have no single source.
  - **Recommendation:** (1) no ADDITIONAL row of a type → nothing of that type is ended or replaced; the T2 increase check for that type is skipped (the engine's DOWNGRADE already refuses increases and is the authority, S6-16 rule 4); (2) several rows → the base included amount comes from the snapshot of the **oldest** remaining row of that type (the original purchase), counted once; the LIFO reduction of storage works in GB with each row's own `unit_quantity` (rows kept oldest first while their GB fit the target additional GB; the last kept row gets ceil(remaining GB / its own `unit_quantity`) blocks), so different block sizes are never mixed into one row. Alternatives: (a) extend `FulfilledOrder` with the engine's `current` included users / GB (the values PHASE_07 passed to the engine) and use them as the base; (b) read the base from the currently ACTIVE USER_SEAT / STORAGE plan version (S5-10 literal) instead of snapshots.
- **State:** P06-T2 IN_PROGRESS (claimed), implementation not resumed; no code, test, configuration or schema change; tracker unchanged 39 / 0 / 1 / 3 / 1 / 98. Docs: plan §3c + T2 status, tracker P06-T2 note, this WORK_LOG. Backup: agent scratchpad `backup-p06-t2-s617/`.

## 2026-10-09 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P06-T2 S6-19 recorded; readiness NOT READY (S6-20) (progress note; no code written)

- **Owner decision:** "OWNER DECISION — S6-19" — option (a) with additional rules, recorded in PHASE_06 plan §3c: explicit current `includedUsers` / `includedStorageGb` in the downgrade data; no ADDITIONAL row of a type → nothing of that type ended / replaced and no T2 increase check (engine authoritative); several rows → base from the oldest applicable remaining row's snapshot, counted once; LIFO; storage in GB with each replacement row keeping its own `unit_quantity` and snapshot, never merged; S6-16..S6-18 in force.
- **Readiness review (read-only, against `PricingEngine` / `CurrentSubscription`): NOT READY — owner question S6-20 (included-amount source):**
  - Evidence: `CurrentSubscription.includedUsers` is documented as "the users included today (USER_SEAT base + item lines)", `includedStorageGb` "the storage included today"; `PricingEngine.downgrade` uses them only to validate `targetUsers ≤ includedUsers + additionalUsers` (storage likewise) and computes no post-downgrade included amount. So (1) S6-19 rule 1 (explicit current included, already containing the base) and rule 3 (base derived from the oldest ADDITIONAL row's snapshot) are two sources for the same quantity and can disagree; (2) it is not stated whether `additional = max(0, target − included)` uses the included amount **before** the removals or **after** the removed PLAN / DEPENDENCY items' contributions are taken away.
  - **Recommendation:** the explicit input is the single source (engine semantics, S6-19 rule 1 / S6-16 rule 4): `includedUsers` / `includedStorageGb` = the engine's current values (before the downgrade, base + item lines); included after the downgrade = the input − the `included_users` / `included_storage_gb` of the plan-price snapshots of the PLAN / DEPENDENCY items in the removal list; additional = max(0, target − included after), blocks per S6-17 / S6-19 rule 5; rule 3 is then not needed for the base (LIFO and the block-size rule stay). Alternatives: (a) the input already means the included amount **after** the removals (PHASE_07 computes it) and T2 uses it as is; (b) keep rule 3: base from the oldest row's snapshot + the remaining items' snapshot contributions, the explicit input used only as a consistency check (mismatch → 400 `INVALID_SUBSCRIPTION_ORDER`).
- **State:** P06-T2 IN_PROGRESS (claimed), paused; no code, test, configuration or schema change; tracker unchanged 39 / 0 / 1 / 3 / 1 / 98. Docs: plan §3c + T2 status, tracker P06-T2 note, this WORK_LOG. Backup: agent scratchpad `backup-p06-t2-s619/`.

- **Owner decision:** "Owner Decision — S6-20: Included Quantity Source" — modified approach recorded in PHASE_06 plan §3c: the explicit pre-downgrade totals `includedUsers` / `includedStorageGb` (`CurrentSubscription` semantics) are the single source; P06-T2 subtracts, once per removed PLAN / DEPENDENCY item, the `included_users` / `included_storage_gb` of its plan-price snapshot; the base is not derived again from the oldest ADDITIONAL row (S6-19 rule 3 superseded for the base); `additionalUsers = max(0, targetUsers − postRemovalIncludedUsers)`, storage blocks per S6-17 / S6-19; ADDITIONAL rows never subtracted; S6-16..S6-18 unchanged.
- **Readiness review (read-only): NOT READY — owner question S6-21 (required additional quantity above the current one):**
  - Implementable: every item has a NOT NULL `plan_price_id`, so each removed non-ended PLAN / DEPENDENCY row's contribution is known and subtracted once; ADDITIONAL rows are excluded.
  - Open: a removed plan can carry included users / GB, so after the removal the required additional quantity can exceed the current one although the engine accepted the downgrade (its check compares the target with the totals **before** the removal: `targetUsers ≤ includedUsers + additionalUsers`). Example: included 20 (base 10 + plan X 10), additional 0, target 20, remove plan X → post-removal included 10 → `additionalUsers` = 10 > 0 (the same for storage). LIFO reduction cannot increase a quantity; S6-17 rule 6 rejects only a target that increases users / storage; S6-19 rule 2 forbids a separate increase check when no ADDITIONAL row of the type exists. Increasing the additional quantity would need a price (none in a DOWNGRADE, Q-08).
  - **Recommendation:** reject such a downgrade with 400 `INVALID_SUBSCRIPTION_ORDER` ("the target needs more additional users / storage than currently purchased after the removal") — for an existing ADDITIONAL type and also for an absent type (the only T2 check for an absent type, limited to this case; S6-19 rule 2 otherwise stays); the admin lowers the target or buys the extra quantity with an UPGRADE. Alternatives: (a) cap the replacement additional quantity at the current quantity (the effective total ends below the target, silently); (b) leave the additional rows of that type unchanged (no replacement) when the required quantity is not lower than the current one.
- **State:** P06-T2 IN_PROGRESS (claimed), paused; no code, test, configuration or schema change; tracker unchanged 39 / 0 / 1 / 3 / 1 / 98. Docs: plan §3c + T2 status, tracker P06-T2 note, this WORK_LOG. Backup: agent scratchpad `backup-p06-t2-s620/`.
- **Additional owner question S6-22 (found in the same review sweep): changes after a renewal paid ahead.** S6-13 allows a renewal to append a PAID period after the latest one while the current period is still running. Then (1) an UPGRADE is priced and prorated only to the current period end (P05-T5), but its items have no end and would run into the already renewed period without payment; (2) a DOWNGRADE is scheduled for the day after the current period end (S6-16), where the renewal has already created that period's items. **Recommendation:** while a renewed period after the current one exists, UPGRADE and DOWNGRADE are refused with 409 `INVALID_STATUS_TRANSITION` (the renewal fixes the next configuration; changes are made after the new period has started). Alternatives: (a) UPGRADE items get `effective_to` = the current period end (paid part only) and a DOWNGRADE replaces the renewal items of the next period; (b) allow both unchanged (the next period's renewal items and the change coexist).

## 2026-10-09 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P06-T2 independent code review: PASS recommendation (read-only; owner acceptance pending)

- **Request:** owner "P06-T2 — Independent Code Review Before Owner Acceptance" (read-only; no code / doc / status change during the review; do not mark DONE; do not start P06-T3 / P06-T4). Reviewer note: the review was done by the same agent session that implemented P06-T2, with a full line-by-line re-read and independent reruns; the owner may request a review by a fresh session for strict independence.
- **Scope checked against PHASE_06 plan §3c (S6-1..S6-22, TA2-1..TA2-14), the P06-T1 schema and the P05-T5 `PricingEngine` contract:** `SubscriptionService` (all operations), `SubscriptionStateMachine`, `SubscriptionPeriodCalculator`, `SubscriptionNumberAllocator`, the repository lock queries, `PlatformSubscriptionService`, the controller diff against the backup, the entitlement reader (`ModuleEntitlementService`, `SubscriptionResolver`, `PlanEntitlementRepository`), every caller of the removed endpoints (none outside CP and docs), file deletions / renames (none; the request DTOs are present).
- **Result: PASS recommendation — no blocking defect.** Every S6 / TA2 rule PASS (decision matrix in the review report): S6-20 matches the engine (`PricingEngine` counts one included contribution per line, not per quantity); S6-21 rejects with and without an ADDITIONAL row (the no-row branch verified by code trace); the downgrade validates everything before mutating; numbering, row locks, idempotency and rollback are correct for standalone transactions (NEW and numbering concurrency proven by `SubscriptionServiceMySqlIT`).
- **Findings (non-blocking):**
  - **R-1** `SubscriptionService.applyRenewal` (EXPIRED renewal): SCHEDULED rows dated in the expired gap (e.g. downgrade replacements) become ENDED with `effective_to` = the old period end < `effective_from` — inconsistent dates, no functional effect (ENDED; no DB check); reachable only once P06-T3 expires subscriptions. Recommended: end never-started SCHEDULED rows without changing their dates. **Owner decision: small T2 fix now or with P06-T3.**
  - **R-2** `scheduleDowngrade` (and the renewal item loop) treat only ACTIVE items as live; until P06-T3 activates due SCHEDULED items at period boundaries a downgrade in a later period ignores them (possible false 400 / "plan not held"). By design ("applied by T3") — **recorded as a P06-T3 prerequisite** (tracker P06-T3 row, plan T3).
  - **R-3** idempotency checks run as plain reads after the row lock; correct in a fresh transaction, but a PHASE_07 caller that already read the same rows in its transaction (REPEATABLE READ snapshot / Hibernate first-level cache) could miss a concurrent duplicate — **PHASE_07 integration note** (own transaction or locking read / unique key).
  - **R-5** without an ADDITIONAL row and without removals T2 still rejects a target above the included amount (400), although S6-19 rule 2 leaves that check to the engine; no behavioural difference with engine-valid input — **owner acknowledgement**.
  - Minor: `SubscriptionResolver` (legacy, replaced by T4) uses the system time zone; correct-dates does not sync periods / `renewal_date` (S6-4 data fix).
  - Untested branches (code-trace correct): no current period 409; S6-21 without an ADDITIONAL row; increase without removals; mixed block sizes on a newer partial row; UPGRADE / RENEWAL underpayment; duplicate UPGRADE / RENEWAL concurrency; IST date boundary; the R-1 scenario — tests recommended.
- **O-T2-1..O-T2-8:** O-T2-1, O-T2-2, O-T2-4, O-T2-5, O-T2-7 verified non-blocking; O-T2-3 conforms to TA2-14; O-T2-6 pre-existing since P06-T1 (same 16 H2 warnings in the P06-T1 unit logs); **O-T2-8 verified — required follow-up P06-T4:** `ModuleEntitlementService.entitledModules` reads only `findByPlanVersionId(subscription.getPlanVersionId())`; order-created subscriptions have `plan_version_id` NULL and `tbl_plan_entitlement.plan_version_id` is NOT NULL → no module until T4 (fail-closed, no unintended access). Recorded in plan S6-5 / T4 and now also in the tracker P06-T4 row.

## 2026-10-09 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P06-T4 readiness review: NOT READY (read-only; owner questions T4-1..T4-8)

- **Scope:** plan T4 (`EntitlementEngine.recompute`, sources PLAN_MODULE / PLAN_BUNDLE / DEPENDENCY / PLATFORM_CORE / MANUAL_GRANT, mandatory closure, diff-apply with `charged_guard`, status from subscription + company, `ADD_MODULE` enqueue, publish after commit, `ManualGrantService` + `POST …/companies/{code}/entitlements/manual-grants`, replaces `ModuleEntitlementService`), PHASE_06 §6.3 / §13 acceptance 2, E6-1 / E6-2, S6-5 / O-T2-8, T3-1..T3-7. Read-only: no code, test, status, DB, Redis, Keycloak or SVN action; no test run.
- **Evidence:** `ModuleEntitlementService.entitledModules` reads `PlanEntitlement` by the subscription's `plan_version_id` through `SubscriptionResolver.validActiveSubscriptions` (ACTIVE, start ≤ today ≤ end, `LocalDate.now()` system zone); consumers `InternalCompanyController` (internal modules API), `ModuleEntitlementCachePublisher` (Redis `COMPANY_MODULES`) and `ModuleDependencyService.impactOf`; order-created subscriptions have no `plan_version_id` (O-T2-8). `CompanyEntitlement`, `EntitlementSourceType`, `EntitlementStatus` (PENDING, ACTIVE, DEGRADED, SUSPENDED, ENDED) and the `charged_guard` callback exist (P06-T1). `ProvisioningJobType` = RESOURCE, RECOVERY only (`ADD_MODULE` belongs to PHASE_08 P08-T1 / T7). `DEGRADED` is set by provisioning / schema upgrade (master §16.5). Gateway `CompanyContextFilter` exempts CONTROL-PANEL and NOTIFICATION but checks FILE; the legacy reader never publishes PLATFORM_CORE modules. Resources reach VERIFIED via `CompanyResourceRegistry`. `CompanyEntitlementChangedEvent` is emitted by company lifecycle, provisioning, `PlatformSubscriptionService` and `SubscriptionService`. 7 test classes seed legacy plan entitlements (`ModuleEntitlementMySqlIT`, `ModuleEntitlementServiceTest`, `ModuleEntitlementSchedulerMySqlRedisIT`, `ModuleCatalogConsumersMySqlIT`, `SubscriptionLifecycleMySqlRedisIT`, `ControlPanelArchitectureIntegrationTest`, `SubscriptionServiceMySqlIT`).
- **Result: NOT READY** — owner questions (recommendation first):
  - **T4-1 legacy reader cutover (O-T2-8 / S6-5):** (a) full cutover — `tbl_company_entitlement` is the only source for the three consumers; legacy plan-version subscriptions (old development DBs only, E6-6) get no entitlement; the 7 test classes are re-seeded with items; the legacy classes are deleted only in P06-T8 — recommended; (b) interim dual read (union of both).
  - **T4-2 contributing subscriptions / items before P06-T3 (T3-1 order):** (a) date-based — a subscription contributes while ACTIVE and a PAID period contains today (Asia/Kolkata); an item counts while not ENDED and its effective range contains today (consistent with T3-3; works before T3 activates / ends items) — recommended; (b) status-based (ACTIVE items only) — entitlements of an advance renewal would be missing at the boundary until T3.
  - **T4-3 entitlement status:** (a) ACTIVE = subscription ACTIVE and company ACTIVE and (module needs no database, or a VERIFIED DATABASE resource exists); PENDING = database not yet VERIFIED; SUSPENDED = subscription SUSPENDED or company not ACTIVE (except ARCHIVED → ENDED); ENDED = source gone (expired / cancelled / item ended / grant expired); DEGRADED never set by the engine and preserved; only ACTIVE rows are published — recommended (behaviour change: the legacy reader publishes without a resource check); (b) another mapping.
  - **T4-4 PLATFORM_CORE:** (a) every catalog PLATFORM_CORE module (file, notification, control-panel) — non-charged, `source_key` `CORE` — while the company is ACTIVE and has at least one contributing subscription; published (FILE then passes the gateway check) — recommended; (b) for every ACTIVE company even without a subscription.
  - **T4-5 `source_key` values (E6-1):** (a) PLAN_MODULE / PLAN_BUNDLE / DEPENDENCY with an item = `ITEM:<subscription_item_id>`; an INCLUDED dependency without an item = `DEP:<parent item id>`; PLATFORM_CORE = `CORE`; MANUAL_GRANT = `GRANT:<n>` (n = 1 + earlier manual grants of that company and module) — recommended; (b) other values.
  - **T4-6 manual grants:** (a) Super Admin; module code, reason (required, ≤ 500) and `valid_to` (required, ≥ today); non-charged; one open grant per company + module (a second → 409); counts while today ≤ `valid_to`, ENDED by the recompute afterwards; no revoke endpoint in T4 (not in the plan); audited — recommended; (b) other rules (e.g. a revoke endpoint).
  - **T4-7 mandatory-closure violation at recompute** (an entitled module whose MANDATORY dependency is not entitled, e.g. after a catalog change): (a) the recompute does not fail — the violation is logged and audited (warning), the rows stay as computed, nothing is granted automatically; the P04-T6 impact report shows it — recommended; (b) the triggering transaction fails (409).
  - **T4-8 `ADD_MODULE` before PHASE_08:** (a) T4 enqueues nothing; a module that needs a database stays PENDING until a VERIFIED resource exists, and PHASE_08 derives its `ADD_MODULE` work from PENDING entitlements — recommended; (b) T4 adds an `ADD_MODULE` job type and records jobs (touches PHASE_08-owned provisioning).
- **Technical assumptions (no business rule; to confirm with the implementation approval):** A4-1 recompute inside the transaction that emits `CompanyEntitlementChangedEvent` (before commit) and publish after commit, also after manual grants and catalog changes; A4-2 a `charged_guard` conflict (two charged sources for one module, acceptance 2) rejects the triggering transaction (the engine validates before insert; the DB guard backs it); A4-3 engine dates in Asia/Kolkata from the injectable `Clock`.
- **Carry-over (T3-6):** the exact uniqueness key and retry behaviour of the notification-request table are to be defined and tested in the T4 / T5 / T6 design / readiness work — not part of the T4 entitlement scope; proposed for the P06-T6 readiness (closest to T3).
- **Status:** P06-T4 **PENDING**, not claimed; no status changed; tracker 40 / 0 / 0 / 3 / 1 / 98. Docs: plan T4 readiness row, tracker (Last updated, P06-T4 note), NEXT_SESSION_PROMPT, AGENT_HANDOFF, this WORK_LOG. Backup: agent scratchpad `backup-p06t4-ready/`.

Options: (a) the largest base and the largest cap among the applicable prices of the qualifying subscriptions' (period, currency) pairs — recommended; (b) the earliest-started qualifying subscription's price (tie → lowest id); (c) a PHASE_05 publish rule making those values equal across the prices of a version (validator change). Plus no applicable price → recommended base 0 and no cap.
  - **B5-2 (T5-8 / T5-9):** the initial value of a lazily created `tbl_company_usage` row — `UserStatus` is ACTIVE / INACTIVE / SUSPENDED (no INVITED, no REMOVED) and invitations exist only as identity-sync outbox tasks, so "INVITED + ACTIVE" (Q-06) cannot be counted from stored state today. Options: (a) start at 0, authoritative after the PHASE_10 wiring + reconciliation (PHASE_09 for storage) — recommended, no production caller in T5; (b) seed from a COUNT of ACTIVE `tbl_company_user` rows.

## 2026-10-10 — P06-T5 F5-7 resolved: test-report secret exposure removed; IT password via environment variable (status stays REVIEW)

- **Owner instruction:** "P06-T5 — Resolve Test-Report Secret Exposure and Submit for Final Owner Review" (P06-T5 stays REVIEW; no DONE; no P06-T3 / T6; no secret in any output).
- **Cause (investigated locally):** the IT runner passed the local test-MySQL password to Maven as `-Dit.mysql.password=…`; Maven forwards command-line properties into the forked surefire JVM as system properties, and surefire writes every system property of the test JVM into its `TEST-*.xml` reports (`<property name="it.mysql.password" value="…"/>`). The CONTROL-PANEL-SERVICE ITs read the password only from that system property (38 files). Environment variables are not written to the reports.
- **Exposure removed (values never printed):** a structural scan (the XML property and `it.mysql.password=<value>` assignments; a value-based scan is not reliable for this credential because its value also occurs as ordinary text) of every module `target/` and both session scratchpads found **1051** files with an unredacted value — all in the previous session's scratchpad (`evidence-t4corr` 892, `backup-p06t4-corr` 156, `logs` 3); none in a module `target/` (the CONTROL-PANEL-SERVICE `target/` had been rebuilt at 15:22 by a build not started by this agent — a jar was packaged without test reports). The values were replaced in place by `[REDACTED]` (evidence otherwise kept); re-scan: **0**.
- **Configuration change (test code only, CONTROL-PANEL-SERVICE):** new test helper `nyaalkaran.erp.system.platform.ItMySqlPassword` — the password from the environment variable `IT_MYSQL_PASSWORD` (the convention of COMMON `LocalTestMySql`), falling back to the legacy property `it.mysql.password` (unchanged defaults); the 38 IT classes that read `System.getProperty("it.mysql.password"…)` now call `ItMySqlPassword.get(<same default>)` (mechanical replacement + import; no other change). The agent's IT runner (session scratchpad `it-run.ps1`) no longer passes `-Dit.mysql.password`; the password reaches the tests only as the child-process environment variable `IT_MYSQL_PASSWORD` (host / port / user stay `-D`, they are not secret), and the runner now reports how many surefire XML reports carry an `it.mysql.password` property.

## 2026-10-12 — P06-T6 implemented → REVIEW (publisher + `TENANT_STATUS`, resync on, gateway status check)

- **Authorization:** "Explicit Implementation Authorization — P06-T6" (P06-T6 only; CD-36, CD-37, third readiness READY (PASS), IC-1..IC-12; CD-38). Claimed earlier today (entry above). Ends as **REVIEW** — only the owner sets DONE. No other task started.
- **CONTROL-PANEL (code):**
  - new `entitlement/CompanyRuntimePublication` — `@Transactional(REQUIRES_NEW)` `publish(code, mode)`: `findByCompanyCodeForUpdate` is the first statement (IC-2), then one Lua script: `SET TENANT_STATUS:<code> <status> EX ttl`; module key `delete` / `replace` (DEL + SADD + EXPIRE) / `keep`. Modes FULL (ACTIVE → tokens or the reserved `#NONE`; any other status → no module key), STATUS_ONLY (ACTIVE keeps the module key, others delete it), EVICT (status + no module key). Fails closed on an unknown company, no Redis or a module without a catalog token (nothing written).
  - `ModuleEntitlementCachePublisher` rewritten over the publication (class name kept — **deviation** from the plan Files row "CompanyRuntimePublisher", to limit churn): `refresh` / `refreshWithOutcome` (T6-7: acknowledges after the publish, honours the ack), `publish` (listed → EVICT), `publishAfterRecompute(code, generation)` (T6-8 / F2), `publishStatus` (TA6-5), `evict` (module key removed even without the database); **bounded retry (3 attempts, 25 / 50 ms) of a publication that loses an InnoDB lock conflict** (1213 / 1205) — see findings.
  - events: `CompanyEntitlementChangedEvent` (class; records the backlog generation read before the recompute), new `CompanyStatusChangedEvent` (published by `CompanyService.create` and `changeStatus`) + `CompanyStatusPublicationListener` (AFTER_COMMIT, fallback; failure → `EntitlementRecomputeRecovery.publishFailed`); `CompanyEntitlementSyncListener` failure → `publishFailed` (IC-4); `EntitlementRecomputeListener` records the generation.
  - `EntitlementRecomputeRecovery`: `retryNow` (resync, no audit), `publishFailed`, no RECOVERED audit when the ack fails (T6-7). `ModuleEntitlementSyncScheduler.resyncActiveCompanies` covers **all** companies (`CompanyRepository.findAllCompanyCodes`): listed → `retryNow`, else `refresh`, failure → backlog. Method names kept (ScheduledJobPolicy allow-list unchanged).
  - `application.yml`: `spring.data.redis.timeout` / `connect-timeout` `2s` (env overridable), `erp.module-entitlement.sync.enabled` `${MODULE_ENTITLEMENT_SYNC:true}`; `docker-compose.yml` `MODULE_ENTITLEMENT_SYNC: ${MODULE_ENTITLEMENT_SYNC:-true}`; test `application.properties`: resync off and recovery retry 1 h (no background noise in ITs).
- **API-GATEWAY (code):** new `filter/CompanyStatusGate` (ACTIVE allowed; DRAFT / PROVISIONING / PROVISIONING_FAILED / AWAITING_DATA_IMPORT → 503 `COMPANY_NOT_READY`; SUSPENDED → 403 `COMPANY_SUSPENDED` except GET / HEAD on `erp.gateway.company-status.suspended-read-paths` — empty today; DISABLED → 403 `COMPANY_DISABLED`; ARCHIVED / PURGE_APPROVED → 403 `COMPANY_ARCHIVED`; missing / unknown / Redis error → 503 `ENTITLEMENT_UNAVAILABLE`). `CompanyContextFilter`: status first on every company-scoped route; CONTROL-PANEL / NOTIFICATION status-only; module segment must match `^[A-Z][A-Z0-9-]{1,59}$` (else 403 before Redis, IC-6); `hasKey` false → 503; not a member (incl. `#NONE`) → 403 `MODULE_NOT_ENTITLED`; `X-Error-Code` on every refusal. `WebSocketTicketController`: the same status check before a ticket is issued. `application.yml`: Redis timeouts 2 s.
- **COMMON:** unchanged (its filter still answers 403 on a missing key — accepted, IC). `_cleanup_review/` untouched.
- **Findings during implementation (fixed in P06-T6):**
  1. Gateway: the former `onErrorResume` wrapped the forwarded request too, so a backend / routing error became 503 `ENTITLEMENT_UNAVAILABLE` (now with the header, misleading). The lookup is isolated; only Redis errors map to 503 `ENTITLEMENT_UNAVAILABLE` (unit test `anErrorOfTheForwardedRequest_isNotReportedAsAnEntitlementFailure`).
  2. CP: two subscriptions suspended concurrently — the after-commit publication (locks by code: index, then row) deadlocked (1213) with the other subscription transaction (locks the row by id, then the code in the engine); InnoDB chose the publication, which then failed closed (company listed, 503 until a retry). Fixed by a bounded retry of the publication on `PessimisticLockingFailureException` only (the retry re-reads the committed state; any other failure is not retried; the last conflict still lists the company). Evidence: the IT log shows "lost a lock conflict (attempt 1); retrying" and the expected final `#NONE` state.
- **Tests added / changed:** CP — `CompanyRuntimePublicationTest` (lock first, Lua order, `#NONE`, statuses, fail-closed), `ModuleEntitlementCachePublisherTest` (+ lock-conflict retry / no retry otherwise), `CompanyStatusPublicationListenerTest`, `ModuleCatalogTokenFormatTest` (marker never a code / token; settings pinned), `CompanyServiceTest` (status events), recovery / listener / scheduler tests; ITs `EntitlementTransactionWiringMySqlRedisIT` (+5: `#NONE` with TTLs; status publication on suspend / disable / reactivate; failed event publication → backlog → resync recovers; **TA6-3 evidence** — a Redis that accepts but never answers fails the publication within the 2 s timeouts (test 2.5 s), the company lock is released (a waiting `FOR UPDATE` gets it in < 6 s), the company is listed and recovers; 8 concurrent publishers + a suspension end in the committed state), `SubscriptionLifecycleMySqlRedisIT` (`#NONE` / status / TTL expectations), `ModuleEntitlementSchedulerMySqlRedisIT`, `ModuleCatalogConsumersMySqlIT`; fixture `EntitlementFixtures.publisherInNewTransactions`. Gateway — `CompanyStatusGateTest` (matrix, SUSPENDED allow-list, fail closed), `CompanyContextFilterTest` (+8: status matrix on module / CONTROL-PANEL / NOTIFICATION routes, missing status / Redis down, missing key 503 vs `#NONE` 403, encoded marker refused before any lookup, status-only routes, downstream error), context tests seeded with `TENANT_STATUS` (`GatewayJwtValidationTest` +2 real-Redis cases, `GatewayWebSocketTicketTest` +1 `/ws-ticket` status case, registry / customer / rate-limit / `LocalKeycloakGatewayIT`). Test Redis defaults of 19 ITs → `127.0.0.1:16379`.
- **Results (actual, local only — MySQL 192.168.5.141 via the runner, Redis `erp-it-redis` 127.0.0.1:16379, local Keycloak 127.0.0.1:8081):** CP unit **841 / 0**; CP focused ITs 28 / 0 (first run 27 / 1 — finding 2, fixed); **CP IT 264 / 0 / 0 / 0** (with local Keycloak); gateway unit **154 / 0** (intermediate failures were the expected constructor / stub updates and finding 1); service `*ModuleEntitlementEnforcementIT` 15 × 6 = **90 / 0**; `LocalKeycloakGatewayIT` **9 / 0 / 0 skipped**; unit reactor **still running when these documents were written (finished so far: COMMON 288 / 0, API-GATEWAY 154 / 0, COMMON-ATTACHMENT 46 / 0, ASSET 17 / 0, COMPLIANCE 188 / 0); the final result follows in a WORK_LOG addendum**.
