- **A5 (N1) implementation:** `CompanyRuntimePublication` — new mode `FULL_UNLESS_LISTED`, new `publishUnlessListed(code)` (REQUIRES_NEW) returning `Published(modules, evicted)`; the backlog is read **only after** `findByCompanyCodeForUpdate` (listed → EVICT: status written, module key deleted, entitlements not read); constructor takes the `EntitlementRecomputeBacklog`. `ModuleEntitlementCachePublisher.publish()` and the not-listed path of `refreshWithOutcome()` use it (an eviction there → `RefreshOutcome(∅, wasListed=true, acknowledged=false)`); the bounded lock-conflict retry is shared (`withLockRetry`, unchanged policy: `PessimisticLockingFailureException`, 3 attempts). `publishStatus`, `publishAfterRecompute` and the listed refresh unchanged (STATUS_ONLY never writes rows; the others publish FULL deliberately and re-evict on a renewed entry). Known limitation documented (class Javadocs, `EntitlementRecomputeRecovery`, PHASE_06 §9): the backlog is per CONTROL-PANEL instance — the cross-instance race remains, bounded by the next successful retry, the resync (120 s) and the TTL (300 s).
- **A5 tests:** new `ModuleEntitlementBacklogRaceTest` (deterministic latch: the real publisher + publication, the locking read held on a latch, the company listed while it waits → `delete`, no entitlement read, refresh outcome not a recovery; control without the listing → `replace`); `CompanyRuntimePublicationTest` +3; `ModuleEntitlementCachePublisherTest` adapted +1; MySQL IT `EntitlementTransactionWiringMySqlRedisIT.aCompanyListedWhileItsPublicationWaitsForTheRowLock_staysEvicted` (a real InnoDB row lock held by the test; the wait detected through `performance_schema.data_lock_waits` by blocker — `INNODB_TRX` did not list this wait, MySQL state "statistics"). **Negative checks** (pre-fix ordering re-introduced in the publisher, file restored byte-identical by SHA-256): unit race test 2 / 3 fail (stale `["crm"]`), MySQL IT fails (stale `["portal","control-panel"]`). Fixture / constructor updates: `EntitlementFixtures`, `ModuleCatalogConsumersMySqlIT`.
- **N2a evidence (`PublicationLockConflictMySqlIT`, new; context with `innodb_lock_wait_timeout = 1` per session; a counting wrapper between the real publisher and the real Spring-proxied publication):**
  - 1205 (row held by the test): 3 attempts, each `org.springframework.dao.PessimisticLockingFailureException` ← `org.hibernate.PessimisticLockException` ← `com.mysql.cj.jdbc.exceptions.MySQLTransactionRollbackException`, error code **1205**, SQL state 40001; the publisher throws the third; ~3 s.
  - 1213 (deterministic: per attempt a fresh holder transaction changes 5 ballast rows, locks the row by id, waits until the publication is blocked on it, then locks the code index entry the publication holds → InnoDB rolls back the lighter publication): 3 attempts, each `org.springframework.dao.CannotAcquireLockException` ← `org.hibernate.exception.LockAcquisitionException` ← `MySQLTransactionRollbackException`, error code **1213**, SQL state 40001; holders rolled back.
  - Exact types / codes / states pinned in the assertions; stable in 3 runs. Lock order and retry policy unchanged. **SQL-code-only retry is supported by the chain** (both carry the Connector/J `SQLException` with vendor code 1205 / 1213) — not implemented (owner decision).

## 2026-10-12 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P06-T6-F1 implemented → REVIEW (CD-39)

- **Scope (CD-39):** N2b company primary-key lock order at all six lock sites + retry eligibility restricted to MySQL vendor codes 1205 / 1213 (max 3 attempts). P06-T2 / P06-T4 / P06-T5 code paths touched; their DONE records unchanged. P06-T6 stays REVIEW.
- **Implementation (CONTROL-PANEL-SERVICE main):**
  - new `service/company/CompanyIdResolver`: `resolve(code)` through a `TransactionTemplate` with `NOT_SUPPORTED` + read-only (the caller's transaction is suspended, the read runs on its own connection, no read view in the caller), cached `ConcurrentHashMap` (code immutable: `updatable = false`), misses never cached; `lockForUpdate(code)` = `findByIdForUpdate(id)` + code revalidation (mismatch → `IllegalStateException`, vanished row → `ResourceNotFoundException`, both drop the cache entry); creation fallback: only a code registered by `CompanyService.create` in the transaction active right now (transaction resource + synchronization: unbound while suspended, rebound on resume, dropped at completion) is locked by its code; every other miss fails closed; `inCallerTransaction(...)` test seam for fixtures over uncommitted data.
  - `CompanyRepository.findIdByCompanyCode`; `findByCompanyCodeForUpdate` now used only by the creation fallback.
  - L1 `CompanyRuntimePublication` (+ `resolveCompany(code)`, called by the non-transactional publisher **before** the REQUIRES_NEW transaction); L2 `EntitlementEngine.lockCompany` (signature unchanged → `ManualGrantService`, `AllowanceService`, `CompanyUsageService` unchanged; the unused `CompanyRepository` dependency removed); L3 `SubscriptionService.lockCompany`; L4 unchanged (by id); L5 `CompanyService.lockCompany` (+ `registerCreated` in `create`); L6 `CompanyProvisioningClaims.lock` (unused repository field removed).
  - `ModuleEntitlementCachePublisher.withLockRetry`: retries only a `PessimisticLockingFailureException` whose cause chain (≤ 10 levels, identity cycle-safe) holds an `SQLException` with vendor code 1205 or 1213; otherwise thrown at once with a WARN carrying only the exception type and the vendor codes (no message payload). Max 3 attempts, backoff unchanged.
- **Deviation from the proposal (equivalent guarantee):** only L1 resolves literally before its transaction starts; L2 / L3 / L5 / L6 run inside caller transactions that begin upstream, so the resolution suspends that transaction (`NOT_SUPPORTED`, own connection) — proven to create no snapshot by the IC-2 IT and its control.
- **New-company fallback — evidence:** the only application path creating a company is `CompanyService.create` (own `@Transactional`; `PlatformOnboardingService.createCompany` adds none), which calls no lock site; status publication runs after commit. So no application path needs the fallback today; it is proven safe where it applies (same transaction owns the new row and its index entry — no wait) and refused elsewhere (nested REQUIRES_NEW and other transactions fail closed without waiting).
- **Tests:** new `CompanyIdResolverTest` (9), `CompanyIdResolverMySqlIT` (6: IC-2 + control, own connection / pool unchanged / caller resumed, creation fallback + nested REQUIRES_NEW refused in < 3 s, another transaction's uncommitted company refused without waiting, cache + revalidation + vanished row on MySQL); `PublicationLockConflictMySqlIT` reworked (1205: exactly 3 attempts, pinned chain unchanged; former 1213 interleaving: **1 attempt, 0 lock failures**, Redis written; control with the old code-index locking read: **still 1213**); wiring IT: new L5 vs subscription-suspension test (deterministic pause hook, lock wait on the suspension's connection observed) and a 0-retry log assertion on the concurrent-suspension test; `ModuleEntitlementCachePublisherTest` (+3: refused codes / no cause / 40001 alone → 1 attempt; chain bounded and cycle-safe; resolution before the publication); 13 test files adapted (constructors, stubs via `TestCompanies.stubRowLock`).
- **Negative check (evidence, not assumption):** `CompanyIdResolver.lockForUpdate` temporarily took the old code-index lock (file restored byte-identically, SHA-256): the L5 test **errored with 1213** (`CannotAcquireLockException`), the concurrent-suspension test **failed** (publisher retries reappeared), the former-1213 test **errored with 1213**; the control passed in both modes.
- **One IT failure found and fixed in the fixture (no assertion changed):** `SubscriptionLifecycleMySqlRedisIT` deletes `CMP_901` after each test and re-creates the same code with a new id; the application resolver bean (shared by `EntitlementFixtures.publisherInNewTransactions` across tests) still had the old id → the lock failed closed ("Company not found: CMP_901; listed for recovery") as designed; a cascading duplicate-resource error followed. The application never reuses a code (sequence), so the fixture now gives each publisher its own real resolver.
- **Results (local only; MySQL 192.168.5.141, Redis 127.0.0.1:16379, local Keycloak 127.0.0.1:8081; no login passed to Maven):** affected unit classes 138 / 0; **CP unit 860 / 0**; **CP IT 275 / 0 / 0 / 0** (second run, after the fixture fix; first run 273 + 1 F + 1 E as above); F1 concurrency ITs 20 / 0 three times; in the whole CP IT run: 0 × 1213, 3 × 1205 and 2 retry warnings (all from the 1205 test, by design), 0 refused lock exceptions. Secret scanner clean before and after every run; run-created databases dropped (CP IT 65 / 65, targeted runs 3 / 3, 2 / 2), 6 / 6 protected databases intact; Redis 0 keys before / after; 0 protected-host mentions.
- **Remaining limitations:** the cache is per instance and never refreshed except on mismatch / vanished row (safe: codes immutable and never reused); a cache miss holds a second pooled connection briefly; the creation fallback has no application caller today; the N1 backlog remains per instance (unchanged).
- **Status:** P06-T6-F1 → **REVIEW** (tracker 42 / 2 / 0 / 3 / 1 / 95 = 143); P06-T6 stays REVIEW. No `.env`, SVN, `_cleanup_review/` change; no protected host touched; nothing marked DONE.

## 2026-10-13 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — P06-T3-F1 review notes N1–N4 + E1 + evidence preservation (docs only)

- **Source:** the read-only self-review of P06-T3-F1 (by the implementing agent — not an independent review; verdict READY FOR OWNER DECISION) and the owner instruction to complete the proposed documentation-only updates (no test, no code change).
- **N1 — changed error-reporting order of `PlanVersionService.create` (successful requests unchanged):** before P06-T3-F1 the plan was resolved and locked first ("Plan not found" 404, then the RETIRED-plan refusal of `PlanService.requireNotRetired`), then the contract / company combination was checked and the company read. Now: missing request → contract / company combination (400 `INVALID_PLAN_DATA`) → contract company lock (unknown company 400 `INVALID_PLAN_DATA`; code mismatch see N3) → plan lock ("Plan not found") → RETIRED-plan refusal → the rest unchanged. Example: a contract request naming an unknown company for a RETIRED plan now answers 400 instead of the RETIRED refusal. No test was added (not authorized).
- **N2 — race test accepted:** `PlanVersionLockOrderMySqlIT` models every subscription change by a `tbl_plan_version` insert (FK `plan_id`) while the company row is held; it takes the same shared lock on the same `tbl_plan` row as `fk_subscription_item_plan_id`. Accepted by the owner for P06-T3-F1. A deterministic race on the real `SubscriptionService` path is tracked for PHASE_07 T7 (plan T7 Tests row); PHASE_07 stays LOCKED, no status changed.
- **N3 — fail-closed company-code mismatch:** `CompanyIdResolver.lockForUpdate` locks the row by primary key and then compares its code with the requested code; on a mismatch it drops the cached id, logs an error and throws `IllegalStateException` — `create` records a FAILED audit and rethrows (a server error; no plan version is written). This is the CD-39 (P06-T6-F1) revalidation rule — the control plane never acts on another company; with `company_code` not updatable it is not expected to occur. Before P06-T3-F1 `create` read the company by code inside the transaction and had no such path.
- **N4 — short company X-lock trade-off:** creating a contract version holds the company row's exclusive lock (Q-1 (a), the existing `findByIdForUpdate`) from the start of `create` until its commit (plan group locks, version and price inserts, audit). Meanwhile other company-row lockers of the same company (subscription changes, lifecycle transitions, publication) wait. Accepted because contract version creation is a rare Super Admin action and Q-1 (a) avoids a new `FOR SHARE` query; standard versions take no company lock.
- **E1 — source hashes (SHA-256; from the agent scratchpad manifest `backup-p06t3f1-SHA256.txt`, recomputed 2026-10-13 and confirmed by the owner's copy):** previous source used for the negative run (`clean test`): `PlanVersionService.java` `6df60e70fe3c82a52ca931e2cd5ca6658f17e975fc74e4f37b5abf8c30fe7574`, `PlanServiceTest.java` `36218297aee3f154e8e3bcbc2b332185c17434179c67ff868b2e9b6bec19494c`; corrected source (restored byte-identical after the negative run; equal to the working copy): `PlanVersionService.java` `c039e95be1211f6e84580c41f78dd2225c8738b82e742c2570ab22d1840ed49f`, `PlanServiceTest.java` `1aad030aea8a6155344b9e3059c1c8516aaa8b2733c2971fe1bcbdaf7bec1886`; new `PlanVersionLockOrderMySqlIT.java` `1a61901be7defdc8553314e3bbf8a01a9c7d173dbd415aa94ffd66e9bfdd0e54`.
- **Evidence preservation (owner-run, owner-reported output reviewed by the agent):** `C:\SVN-Evidence\P06-T3-F1\` (owner-run copy, verified: 14 files — `test-logs\` 10, `manifests\` 2, `negative-run-source\` 2; `fc /b` 14 / 14 identical; SHA-256 list `C:\SVN-Evidence\P06-T3-F1-evidence-SHA256.txt`). - **Duplicate-code detection (read-only code analysis): reliable at issuance within the T2 scope, no PHASE_05 or schema change.** `ConfigStatus` = DRAFT / APPROVED / RETIRED; APPROVED versions are immutable except the audited `effective_to` of a supersede (`VersionedConfigApprovalService.java:15-23`, `SellerEntityService.java:213-218`), so superseded versions stay APPROVED with their code; RETIRED only for DRAFT or not-yet-effective versions (`SellerEntityService.java:191-199`), which never issued; no main code deletes seller rows; approvals lock all seller rows (`SellerEntityRepository.lockAll`). Rule: refuse when any APPROVED row of a different seller `code` has the issuing seller's `invoice_series_code` (any window; DRAFT / RETIRED ignored; same-seller versions excluded) — a T2-owned read-only query. Race: the later-approved of two sellers starts every issuance after both approvals, always sees the other and is refused; two sellers never allocate from one series. Consequence: such a code stays blocked for both sellers (APPROVED history is permanent); recovery is a new seller version with a unique code through PHASE_05.
- **P07-T2 readiness (fourth): NOT READY — one small owner decision.** **T2-R4:** the error for a detected duplicate is not specified — (a) reuse 409 `INVOICE_SERIES_CODE_INVALID` with a duplicate reason, or (b) a separate 409 `INVOICE_SERIES_CODE_DUPLICATE` (recommended); plus confirmation of the duplicate definition (literal "approved seller entities", superseded APPROVED versions included). - **Recorded as CD-60** (`PHASE_01_SAAS_FOUNDATION.md` §0.1, after CD-59; earlier entries unchanged).
- **A-4 inspection before the change (R-1):** `PricingQuoteService.calculate` is `@Transactional(readOnly = true)`; it calls the proxied `GstComponentResolver.resolve` (`@Transactional(readOnly = true)`, propagation REQUIRED → it joins the caller's transaction) once per tax key and catches `TaxConfigException` (`PricingQuoteService.java:364-376`). A `TaxConfigException` leaving the inner interceptor marked the shared transaction rollback-only, so a calculation whose failed key no line uses ended in `org.springframework.transaction.UnexpectedRollbackException: Transaction silently rolled back because it has been marked as rollback-only` at the commit of `calculate` — **proven by a negative run** with the new IT before the fix (`cd60-negative-before-fix`: `PricingQuoteMySqlIT` 6 tests, 1 error, exactly this exception). Per-key failures come from `resolve`'s own body (`rule` / `requireComponents`); `TaxGoLiveGate.requireReady` (also proxied, default rollback) runs in `calculate` before the resolution for the same date, so it cannot fail inside `resolve` once it passed; outside production it is a no-op. `resolve` only reads (seller, tax rules, gate) and writes nothing; `TaxConfigException` has no subclass (private constructor); `resolve`'s only caller is `PricingQuoteService`.
- **A-4 change (backups `backup-cd60-code\` + SHA-256 list):** `pricing/tax/GstComponentResolver.java` — `resolve` is `@Transactional(readOnly = true, noRollbackFor = TaxConfigException.class)` plus javadoc (one exception-specific rule; no broad `noRollbackFor`; every other exception keeps the default rollback rule; the gate unchanged). Tests: `pricing/quote/PricingQuoteMySqlIT` +3 (`r1_aFailedTaxKeyThatNoLineUses_neitherFailsTheCalculation_norRollsBackTheCaller` — only an APPROVED MODULE INTRA rule, the user-seat / storage keys fail, a modules-only calculation succeeds standalone and inside a calling read-write transaction whose own write commits; `r1_aFailedTaxKeyThatALineUses_isStill409_andRollsBackTheCallerCompletely` — 409 `TAX_CONFIGURATION_NOT_APPROVED` and the caller's write rolled back, no partial write; `r1_theResolverAlone_stillRefusesAMissingRule_withItsOwn409`) and `pricing/tax/GstComponentResolverTest` +1 (`r1_onlyTheTaxConfigurationRefusal_leavesASurroundingTransactionUsable` — exactly `noRollbackFor = {TaxConfigException}`, read-only, REQUIRED, no other rollback rule; the gate has none). Diff: 1 annotation + javadoc in main; nothing else under any `src` changed. Pricing formulas, policy / catalog / tax resolution and backward compatibility unchanged.
- **Verification (runner `it-run-corr.ps1`; secret scanner by absolute path exit 0 before and after every run; only run-created databases dropped, back to the pre-flight 6; 0 Redis keys; 0 protected-host references):** negative run before the fix — see above; `cd60-focused` 69 / 0 / 0 / 0 (11 classes: `PricingQuoteMySqlIT` 6, `GstComponentResolverTest` 9, `TaxConfigurationMySqlIT` 7, `PricingSchemaMySqlIT` 4, `PricingQuoteServiceTest` 10, `TaxConfigServicesTest` 12, `TaxConfigValidatorTest` 6, `TaxGoLiveGateTest` 4, `TaxSampleSeederTest` 4, `VersionedConfigApprovalServiceTest` 6, `SamplePricingPolicyParityTest` 1); `cd60-cp-unit` 952 / 0 (951 + 1); `cd60-cp-full-it` (local Keycloak 127.0.0.1:8081) 329 tests in 55 classes (326 + 3 new), 8 local-Keycloak classes, 328 passed and **1 error**: `BillingAccountMySqlIT.anInvoice_locksTheTaxIdentity_butNotTheEmail` — `Duplicate entry '773' for key 'tbl_billing_policy.uk_billing_policy_version'` from the P07-T3 test fixture's random policy version (`700 + hash % 99`, `BillingAccountMySqlIT.java:519`), a pre-existing flaky fixture collision unrelated to A-4 (not fixed — P07-T3 file, out of scope); rerun `cd60-billing-account-rerun` 10 / 0. ## 2026-10-16 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — CONTROL-PANEL-SERVICE request DTO validation (service-invoked) → REVIEW
- **Scope / design (owner decisions D1–D7, 2026-10-16):** D1 option B — request constraints live on the DTOs in the validation group `shared.validation.ServiceValidation` and are checked by the owning service at its existing validation point, inside its audited block, through `RequestConstraints.check` / `checkMessage` (first violation in the established check order, message `"<field path>: <message>"` or the service's own message, never the rejected value). Error codes, FAILED / DENIED audits and 404 / 409 precedence are unchanged; `@Valid` stays only where it already was (default group). Trim / strip semantics, truncation (subscription and dependency-removal reasons), decimal normalization and the Asia/Kolkata clock rules are preserved. New flat package `shared/validation`: `ServiceValidation`, `Whitespace`, `RequiredText`, `TrimmedSize`, `TrimmedPattern`, `DecimalDigits` (+ validators; Hibernate Validator `@Digits` measures the raw scale, the services measure after `stripTrailingZeros`), `RequestFormats` (pinned by tests to COMMON `TenantResourceNaming.COMPANY_CODE` and the catalog patterns), `RequestConstraints`. CP `AGENTS.md` package-layout line updated.
- **Inventory:** 42 request DTO files + 2 nested types (44), 59 DTO-body endpoints, 5 live legacy `Map` bodies. **Moved to DTO constraints (13 files / 15 types):** `OrderCreateRequest`, `OrderUpdateRequest`, `OrderActionRequest` (reason checked for cancel only), `OrderAdjustmentsRequest` (+ `Adjustment`), `ChangeOrderRequest`, `DowngradeCancelRequest`, `RenewalCreateRequest`, `CreditNoteRequest` (+ `Line`), `ManualPaymentRecordRequest`, `PaymentActionRequest` (required reason for reject / reverse stays in the service), `PricingCalculateRequest`, `CreatePricingPlanRequest`, `CreateModuleRequest`; services `OrderRequests`, `ChangeService`, `RenewalService`, `CreditNoteService`, `PaymentRequests`, `PricingQuoteService`, `PlanService.create`, `ModuleCatalogService.create` (inline field checks removed only where a constraint now covers them exactly; cross-field and state rules stay in the services). **Boundary fixes (2):** `UpdateCompanyProfileRequest.countryCode` (`@TrimmedSize(2, 2)`: blank clears again, as documented; padded code accepted); `UserCreateRequest` duplicate getter / setter `@NotBlank` removed (one violation). **Unchanged by decision / evidence (27):** billing account, plan version / prices, tax rule / component / seller / policy / approval (dedicated domain validators), `CreateCompanyRequest`, `UpdatePricingPlanRequest`, `UpdateModuleRequest`, module dependency add / remove, Assign / Invite member, the 3 platform-admin DTOs (limits 20 / 500 kept), the 2 entitlement DTOs, `CreateBundleRequest` / `UpdateBundleRequest`, `SubscriptionActionRequest`, `LoginDiscoveryRequest` (anti-enumeration), `CompanyLifecycleRequest` (an over-long reason is refused today by the audited DB fallback 400 `INVALID_VALUE` — read from the code, not verified live).
- **COMMON `GlobalExceptionHandler`:** new 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` handlers for `MissingServletRequestParameterException` (was an unhandled 500 — affects every servlet service) and `HandlerMethodValidationException` (parameter name + constraint message, no rejected value).
- **Visible contract changes (messages only; codes and statuses unchanged):** list indexes in paths (`planCodes[1]: …`); some combined messages are now per field (`users` / `storageGb`, credit-note `lines`, payment `amount` digits); with several invalid fields the first reported one can differ (e.g. nested adjustments).

## 2026-10-16 — agent (Claude Opus 5.5) — Security-hardening findings from the DTO validation inventory (recorded, not implemented)
- **F1 — legacy `PUT /api/keycloak/{id}`** (`legacy/keycloak/controller/KeycloakController`): the whole `Map` body is forwarded to the Keycloak admin API as the user representation, so a caller could set fields such as `emailVerified`, `credentials` or `requiredActions`; a Keycloak 4xx is swallowed and answered 200. M2M-only, gateway-denied, retired by PHASE_10 / 11.
- **F2 — legacy path variables** (`id`, `realmName`, `clientName`, `roleId`, `userId`) are concatenated raw into Keycloak admin URLs by `KeycloakService` (`clientName` also into a query string).
- **F3 — raw input echoed in error messages:** e.g. "Company not found: X", "Invalid company code: X", unknown `status` / `module` / `serviceName` / `allowanceType` values; COMMON validation / type-mismatch responses also return `rejectedValue`. No length cap on the echoed text.
- **F4 — failure audits:** allowance-override and manual-grant failure audits store the path `companyCode` untruncated as `target_id` (column length 255) — an oversized path value could make the audit insert fail (not verified).
- **F5 — unbounded inputs:** no size limit on request collections (`planCodes`, order `adjustments`, credit-note `lines`, plan `prices`, tax `components`, bundle `modules`, legacy `attributes`) and no JSON body-size limit; owner decision 2026-10-16: no arbitrary limits in the DTO task.
- **Status:** recorded for owner decision; no change made.
